On wave systems with antisymmetric potential in dimension d ≥ 4 d\geq 4 and well-posedness for (half-)wave maps
Silvino Reyes Farina, Armin Schikorra

Abstract
. We prove a priori estimates for wave systems of the type ∂ t ​ t 𝐮 − Δ ​ 𝐮 = Ω ⋅ d ​ 𝐮 + F ⁡ ( 𝐮 ) in ℝ d × ℝ \partial_{tt}{\bf u}-\Delta{\bf u}=\Omega\cdot d{\bf u}+F({\bf u})\quad\text{in $\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}$} where d ≥ 4 d\geq 4 and Ω \Omega is a suitable antisymmetric potential. We show that the assumptions on Ω \Omega are applicable to wave- and half-wave maps, the latter by means of the Krieger-Sire reduction. We thus obtain well-posedness of those equations for small initial data in H ˙ d 2 ​ ( ℝ d ) \dot{H}^{\frac{d}{2}}(\mathbb{R}^{d}) .



.

1991 Mathematics Subject Classification
35L05, 35B40

1.
Introduction

In
[
25
]
Rivière investigated the system

(1.1)

\Delta{\bf u}=\Omega\cdot\nabla{\bf u}\quad\text{in $\mathbb{R}^{2}$}

for maps
{\bf u}:\mathbb{R}^{2}\to\mathbb{R}^{N}
, and showed that if
\Omega
is an antisymmetric
L^{2}
-potential, namely if

\Omega_{ij}=-\Omega_{ji}\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2},\mathbb{R}^{2}),\quad i,j\in\{1,\ldots,N\}

then an adaptation of Uhlenbeck’s Coulomb gauge
[
34
]
leads to compensation phenomena, an effect closely related to the moving frame technique by Hèlein
[
9
,
27
]
. In particular, any
W^{1,2}
-solution
u
to the above equation must be Lipschitz continuous. This has applications, among many other things, to the regularity theory of geometric equations such as the harmonic map equation. The methods have also been extended to half-harmonic maps in
[
4
]
, and several other elliptic settings, e.g.
[
14
,
26
,
3
,
30
,
28
,
29
,
22
,
8
]
.

Motivated by this elliptic theory we investigate the wave-version of (
1.1
). Namely we consider maps
{\bf u}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(-T,T)\to\mathbb{R}^{N}
which solve

(1.2)

\begin{cases}\partial_{tt}{\bf u}-\Delta{\bf u}=\Omega\cdot d{\bf u}+F({\bf u})\quad&\text{in $\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(-T,T)$}\\
{\bf u}(0)=u_{0}\quad&\text{in $\mathbb{R}^{d}$}\\
\partial_{t}{\bf u}(0)=v_{0}\quad&\text{in $\mathbb{R}^{d}$}\end{cases}

Here
\Omega=\left(\Omega_{ij}^{\alpha}\right)_{\alpha\in\{0,\ldots,d\},\ i,j\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\in so(N)\otimes\mathbb{R}^{d}
is antisymmetric

\Omega_{ij}=-\Omega_{ji}\in\mathbb{R}^{d},\quad i,j\in\{1,\ldots,N\}

and we denote

\left(\Omega\cdot d{\bf u}\right)^{i}=\sum_{\alpha=0}^{d}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Omega_{ij}^{\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}u^{j}\quad i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}.

We adapt the convention that the
0
-th derivative is the time derivative, i.e.
\partial_{0}f=\partial_{t}f
.

We focus on the case where
{\bf u}
maps into the sphere
{\mathbb{S}}^{2}\subset\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Our main result is the following a priori estimate

Theorem 1.1
.

There exists
s_{0}>1
such that the following holds for any
s\in(1,s_{0})
. There exists
\varepsilon>0
with the following properties:

Whenever
\Omega
is admissible in the sense of
Definition

2.4
below and
F
is admissible in the sense of
Definition

2.2
, and
u:\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[-T,T]\to{{\mathbb{S}}^{2}}
is a smooth solution of (
1.2
) with initial data bounds

\|{\bf u_{0}}\|_{H^{\frac{d}{2}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|{\bf\dot{u}_{0}}\|_{H^{\frac{d-2}{2}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}<\varepsilon

and

(1.3)

\sup_{t\in(-T,T)}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}<\varepsilon,

then if

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{C^{0}_{t}L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(-T,T))}+\||\nabla|^{s-1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(-T,T))}<\infty

we actually have

(1.4)

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(-T,T))}+\||\nabla|^{s-1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(-T,T))}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}{\bf u_{0}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}{\bf\dot{u}_{0}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.\end{split}

A priori estimates such as the one above, combined with a uniqueness results, can often be used to obtain an existence theory.

And indeed, as a consequence of
Theorem

1.1
we recover in particular the well-posedness result from Shatah-Struwe
[
32
]
for wave maps. Moreover, following the idea of Krieger–Sire
[
13
]
to reduce the half-wave map equation to an equation structurally similar to the wave map equation, we also obtain the following well-posedness result for halfwave maps.

Corollary 1.2

(Global Existence half-wave equation)
.

Let
d\geq 4
. There exists
\varepsilon>0
,
s>1
such that the following holds.

Assume that
{\bf u_{0}}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to{\mathbb{S}}^{2}
such that
{\rm supp\,}\left({\bf u_{0}}-{\bf q}\right)
is compact for some constant vector
{\bf q}\in{\mathbb{S}}^{2}
, and we also assume

(1.5)

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}{\bf u_{0}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}<\varepsilon.

Then there exists a unique solution
{\bf u}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(-\infty,\infty)\to{\mathbb{S}}^{2}
such that

\begin{cases}\partial_{t}{\bf u}={\bf u}\wedge(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{2}}{\bf u}\quad&\text{in $\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(-\infty,\infty)$}\\
{\bf u}(0)={\bf u_{0}}\quad&\text{in $\mathbb{R}^{d}$}\end{cases}

with the estimate for all suitably small
s>1

(1.6)

\begin{split}\||\nabla|^{s-1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(-\infty,\infty))}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}{\bf u_{0}}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\end{split}

Halfwave maps appear in the physics literature as the continuum limit of Calagero-Moser spin systems, see
[
18
]
for details. Their mathematical study has been recently initiated in
[
13
,
16
]
, see also
[
15
,
7
,
1
,
33
]
.

To the best of our knowledge,
Corollary

1.2
is new in dimension
d=4
. Uniqueness was proven for wave maps in
[
32
]
and for halfwave maps in
[
6
]
. In dimension
d\geq 5

[
13
]
, and in dimension
d=4

[
12
]
, a corresponding existence result for half-wave maps was obtained, but under stronger assumptions. Namely instead of the
H^{\frac{d}{2}}
-assumption (
1.5
) the assumed one involving the Besov space
B^{\frac{d}{2},1}_{2}
.
[
20
]
obtained the existence part of
Corollary

1.2
for
d\geq 5
with the condition (
1.5
) not only in the sphere-case
{\mathbb{S}}^{2}
, but also for maps into hyperbolic space.
[
24
]
obtained uniqueness results for the hyperbolic version of halfwave maps.
In
[
19
]
existence in the most interesting case
d=1
is obtained by parabolic approximation. In
[
21
]
further well-posedness in dimension
d=3
was obtained for small initial data in the critical Besov space with additional assumptions angular regularity and weighted decay of the derivative.

Outline.
In
Section

2
we discuss the admissibility conditions in
Theorem

1.1
– these are, besides the antisymmetry of
\Omega
, natural (albeit technical) growth conditions and a curl-condition on
\Omega
. In
Section

3
we follow essentially the spirit of Rivière’s
[
25
]
and use Uhlenbeck’s gauge to transform
\Omega
into a divergence-free vectorfield, that thanks to the
curl
-condition grows quadratically. We then discuss the regularity dependency of the transformed
\Omega
and the gauge, a major technical unpleasantness that takes inspiration from estimates on the Coulomb frame e.g. in Shatah-Struwe’s
[
32
]
for the wave-map equation into general manifolds. In
Section

4
we then obtain the proof of
Theorem

1.1
using a space-time Hodge decomposition, Strichartz estimates (hence the dimension
d\geq 4
), and combining all the previous estimates. In
Section

5
we show that wavemaps into spheres and halfwave maps fall into the form of our theorem. For the wavemaps this is a direct application of the observation in Rivière’s
[
25
]
; for the halfwave map equation this is more work but similar in spirit. Lastly, in
Section

6
we prove the well-posedness result for half-wave maps under small
H^{\frac{d}{2}}
-initial data,
Corollary

1.2
.

Notation:

We work on maps
{\bf u}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}^{N}
(although most of the time we have in mind
{\bf u}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to{\mathbb{S}}^{2}\subset\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Vectorial maps will be written bold-faced.

For presentational reasons we will divide indices of domain vectors in
\mathbb{R}^{n}
in Latin script

a,b,c\in\{1,\ldots,d\},

and Greek script (if time could be considered)

\alpha,\beta,\gamma\in\{0,1,\ldots,d\}.

Coefficients of target vectors in
\mathbb{R}^{N}
are denoted by

i,j,k,\ell\in\{1,\ldots,N\}.

The vector of first order derivatives is

d=(\partial_{t},\partial_{1},\ldots,\partial_{d}).

2.
Growth and compatibility conditions of
\Omega
and
F

In this section we discuss the admissibility conditions assumed in
Theorem

1.1
.
They may look very technical, but the growth conditions in
Definition

2.1
below essentially assume that
\Omega
behaves growth-wise like
udu
In
Definition

2.4
we additionally assume a curl-type growth structure, namely
{\rm curl\,}(\Omega)\approx|\nabla u|^{2}
. Observe this is the case if
\Omega=A(u)\nabla u
.

Definition 2.1

(Growth conditions for
\Omega
)
.

Assume that for any
{\bf u}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[-T,T]\to{{\mathbb{S}}}^{2}

we have

•

For any
s\in[0,\frac{d-4}{2}]
,
p\in(1,\frac{d}{s+1})
and
q\in[1,\infty]

(2.1)

\||\nabla|^{s}d\Omega\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma})\,\||\nabla|^{s+1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})};

For
s\in[0,\frac{d-4}{2}]
,
p\in(1,\frac{d}{s})
and
q\in[1,\infty]

(2.2)

\||\nabla|^{s}\Omega\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma})\,\||\nabla|^{s+1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

In particular we then have

(2.3)

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}d\Omega\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma})\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

and for
q\in[1,\infty]

(2.4)

\|\Omega\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma})\,\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

Definition 2.2

(Perturbative Assumptions of
F
)
.

•

For
t\in[1,\frac{d-2}{2}]
,
\sigma\in[0,1]
such that
\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2t}\in(1,\infty)
,

(2.5)

\||\nabla|^{t-1}F({\bf u})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\left(\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma_{1}}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma_{2}}\right)\||\nabla|^{t}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

and

(2.6)

\|F({\bf u})\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{3},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\left(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma_{2}}\right)\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

•

There exists
\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}>0
such that for any
{\bf u}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to{\mathbb{S}}^{2}
, and any
s>1
we have the following estimate which essentially means
F
is perturbative

(2.7)

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}F({\bf u})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim_{s}\left(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma}\right)\||\nabla|^{s-1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}.

With the growth conditions on
\Omega
and perturbative behavior of
F
we can estimate repeated time derivatives. Namely we have

Lemma 2.3
.

Assume
d\geq 4
and that
F
and
\Omega
satisfy the assumptions of
Definition

2.1
. Assume that

{\bf u}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\times[-T,T]\to{\mathbb{S}}^{2}
solves

\partial_{tt}{\bf u}-\Delta{\bf u}=\Omega\cdot d{\bf u}+F({\bf u})

Then for some
\gamma>0
,

(2.8)

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}dd{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma})\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

and for any
s\in[1,2]

(2.9)

\||\nabla|^{s-2}dd{\bf u}\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma})\||\nabla|^{s-1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

In particular, if (
1.3
) holds then so does

(2.10)

\sup_{t\in(-T,T)}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}dd{\bf u}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}<\varepsilon,

Moreover, there exists a uniform
\varepsilon>0
such that if we assume additionally that

(2.11)

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}dd{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}<\varepsilon

then for any
t\in[1,\frac{d-2}{2}]
,
\sigma\in[0,1]
so that
\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2t}\in(1,\infty)
we have

(2.12)

\begin{split}\||\nabla|^{t-1}dd{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{t}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.\end{split}

Proof.

As for (
2.8
)
we observe that using fractional Leibniz-rule
Lemma

A.1
, (
2.5
) for
\sigma=1
and
t=\frac{d-2}{2}
, then Sobolev embedding

\begin{split}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}dd{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\left(\partial_{tt}-\Delta\right){\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\left(\Omega\cdot d{\bf u}\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}F\left({\bf u}\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\max_{s\in[0,\frac{d-4}{2}]}\||\nabla|^{s}\Omega\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s+1}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4-2s}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{d-2s-2}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\left(\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma_{1}}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma_{2}}\right)\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\Omega\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\left(\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma_{1}}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma_{2}}\right)\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:Omegacond:growth}}{\lesssim}&\left(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\tilde{\gamma}}\right)\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\end{split}

In the last line we also used Young’s inequality
a^{\gamma_{1}}\leq 1+a^{\gamma_{2}}
for
\gamma_{1}<\gamma_{2}
, choosing
\tilde{\gamma}>0
large enough.

As for (
2.9
)
, with a similar argument

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s-2}dd{\bf u}\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{s-2}\left(\Omega\cdot d{\bf u}\right)\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s-2}F({\bf u})\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s-1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\overset{s\leq 2}{\lesssim}&\|\Omega\cdot d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{3},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|F({\bf u})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{3}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s-1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\|\Omega\|_{L^{(d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|F({\bf u})\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{3},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s-1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\Omega\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|F({\bf u})\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{3},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s-1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:Omegacond:growth}}{\lesssim}&\left(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\gamma}\right)\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|F({\bf u})\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{3},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s-1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\end{split}

We conclude with (
2.6
).

As for (
2.12
)

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{t-1}dd{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{t}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{t-1}(\partial_{tt}-\Delta){\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{t}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{t-1}\left(\Omega\cdot d{\bf u}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{t-1}F({\bf u})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:Fcond:2},\eqref{eq:411Ssmallv1}}{\lesssim}&\||\nabla|^{t}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\max_{\tilde{s}\in[0,t-1]}\||\nabla|^{s}\Omega\|_{L^{\frac{d}{1+s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{t-1-s}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2(t-1-s)}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\left(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\Omega\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\||\nabla|^{t}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:Omegacond:growth},\eqref{eq:411Ssmallv1}}{\lesssim}&\||\nabla|^{t}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.\end{split}

∎

The next conditions essentially state that
{\rm curl\,}\Omega=0
– up to perturbative quantities, and that
\Omega_{ij}
is essentially collinear with
\nabla u
, in the sense that
\Omega_{ij}^{\alpha}\partial_{\beta}u=\Omega_{ij}^{\beta}\partial_{\alpha}u
up to perturbative quantities – a condition we call cancellation.

Definition 2.4

(Admissible
\Omega
)
.

For a map
{\bf u}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}\to{\mathbb{S}}^{2}
we assume that
\Omega=\Omega({\bf u})
satisfies the following conditions:

•

Antisymmetry:
\Omega_{ij}^{\alpha}=-\Omega_{ji}^{\alpha}
.

•

\Omega
satisfies the growth conditions of
Definition

2.1
.

•

\Omega
has a gradient-structure up to perturbative quantities, in the sense we have an estimate on its curl:

\partial_{a}\Omega^{b}-\partial_{a}\Omega^{b}=G_{1;a,b}({\bf u})

and

\partial_{a}\Omega^{0}+\partial_{t}\Omega^{a}=G_{1;a,0}({\bf u})

where
G_{1}(u)
satisfies the following estimates, which all would follow if we knew that
G_{1}({\bf u})\approx g({\bf u})|d{\bf u}|^{2}
:

For some
\gamma>0
:

If
s\in[1,\frac{d-2}{2}]
,
p\in(1,\frac{2d}{s+1})
,
q\in[1,\infty]

(2.13)

\||\nabla|^{s-1}G_{1;\alpha,\beta}({\bf u})\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma})\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{s}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

We also have

(2.14)

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-6}{2}}dG_{1;\alpha,\beta}({\bf u})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma})\ \left(\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}dd{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}\right).

and

(2.15)

\|dG_{1;\alpha,\beta}({\bf u})\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{5-\sigma},q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|dd{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{d}{2}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

also for any
q\in[1,\infty]
, and whenever
(1-\sigma)\in[\frac{2}{d-2},1]
,

(2.16)

\|dG_{1;\alpha,\beta}({\bf u})\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{5-\sigma},q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{-1}dd{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\sigma}\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}dd{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}\,\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

(2.17)

\|G_{1;\alpha,\beta}({\bf u})\|_{L^{(d,1)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}.

Whenever
(1-\sigma)\in[\frac{2(s-1)}{d-2},1]
and
s\in[1,\frac{d-2}{2}]

(2.18)

\||\nabla|^{s-1}G_{1;\alpha,\beta}({\bf u})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2-(1-\sigma)}\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}.

Also, for
s\geq 2
and
(1-\sigma)\in[\frac{2(s-1)}{d-2},1]

(2.19)

\||\nabla|^{s-2}dG_{1;\alpha,\beta}({\bf u})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{-1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\sigma}\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}dd{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}.

•

We have the cancellation condition

(2.20)

\begin{cases}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left(\Omega^{a}_{ij}\partial_{b}u^{j}-\Omega^{b}_{ij}\partial_{a}u^{j}\right)=G_{2;a,b}({\bf u})\quad&\text{$a,b\in\{1,\ldots,N\}$}\\
\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left(\Omega^{a}_{ij}\partial_{t}u^{j}+\Omega^{0}_{ij}\partial_{a}u^{j}\right)=G_{2;a,0}({\bf u})\quad&\text{$a\in\{1,\ldots,N\}$}\\
\end{cases}

and for some
\gamma_{1}>0
, with perturbative estimates

(2.21)

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}dG_{2;\alpha,\beta}({\bf u})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma_{1}}\,\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}.

3.
Coulomb gauge estimates

The following is due to Uhlenbeck
[
34
]
and Rivière
[
25
, Lemma A.3]
, see also the presentation e.g. in
[
5
, Theorem 4.2.]
; the choice of
P
is related to the Coulomb gauge and the moving frame used for harmonic maps,
[
27
]
.

For a given
\Omega:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to so(N)\otimes\mathbb{R}^{d}
we set

(3.1)

\Omega_{P}^{\alpha}:=\begin{cases}\partial_{t}PP^{T}+P\Omega^{0}\,P^{T}\quad&\alpha=0\\
-\partial_{\alpha}P\,P^{T}+P\Omega^{\alpha}P^{T}\quad&\alpha\in\{1,\ldots,N\}\end{cases}

Lemma 3.1
.

There exists
\varepsilon>0
and
C>0
depending only on the dimensions
d
,
N
such that the following holds.

Assume
\Omega_{ij}^{a}\in L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
,
i,j\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
and
a\in\{1,\ldots,d\}
with the condition

\|\Omega\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}<\varepsilon.

Then there exists
P\in W^{1,d}(\mathbb{R}^{d},SO(N))
satisfying

(3.2)

\|\nabla P\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\leq C\,\|\Omega\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

and

\sum_{a=1}^{d}\partial_{a}\Omega_{P}^{a}=0,

It is important to observe that this gives (for now) no information about
\Omega^{0}
whatsoever.

In the following we will always assume that
P
is from
Lemma

3.1
and that
\Omega
is admissible in the sense of
Definition

2.4
; we will derive several useful estimates.

Lemma 3.2
.

Assume that
\Omega
is admissible in the sense of
Definition

2.4
and (
2.8
) holds.

There exists a uniform
\varepsilon>0
such that if

(3.3)

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}dd{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}<\varepsilon

then we have

(3.4)

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\Omega_{P}^{a}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}

and

(3.5)

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}P\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

Let us remark that the smallness assumption (
3.3
) is not needed for this specific lemma, an estimate
\lesssim 1
would be enough. But for the overall estimates of the gauge, they are needed, see (
3.15
).

Proof.

Denote by

\mathscr{R}^{\perp}:=|\nabla|^{-1}{\rm curl\,}.

Let
s\in[1,\frac{d}{2}]
. We consider by a slight abuse of notation for
\Omega
only the spatial part, then we have

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s-1}\left(\nabla P^{T}\,P+P\Omega P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{s-1}\underbrace{\mathscr{R}\cdot(\nabla P^{T}\,P+P\Omega P^{T})}_{=0}\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s-1}\mathscr{R}^{\perp}(\nabla P^{T}\,P+P\Omega P^{T})\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{s-1}[\mathscr{R}^{\perp},P_{ij}](\nabla P_{k\ell})\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s-1}\left(\mathscr{R}^{\perp}[P_{ij},\Omega_{k\ell}]P_{mn}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s-1}\left(P\,\mathscr{R}^{\perp}\Omega\,P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\max_{t\in[0,s-1]}\||\nabla|^{\frac{1}{2}+t}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{\frac{1}{2}+t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\||\nabla|^{\frac{1}{2}+s-1-t}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-\frac{1}{2}-t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\max_{t_{1}+t_{2}+t_{3}=s-1}\||\nabla|^{t_{1}+\frac{1}{2}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{t_{1}+\frac{1}{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{t_{2}-\frac{1}{2}}\Omega\|_{L^{\frac{d}{t_{2}-\frac{1}{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{t_{3}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{t_{3}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\max_{t_{1}+t_{2}+t_{3}=s-1}\,\||\nabla|^{t_{1}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{t_{1}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{t_{2}}\mathscr{R}^{\perp}\Omega\|_{L^{\frac{d}{t_{2}+1}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{t_{3}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{t_{3}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{s-\frac{1}{2}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-\frac{1}{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}\\
&+\||\nabla|^{s-\frac{1}{2}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-\frac{1}{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\Omega\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\left(\|P\|_{L^{\infty}}+\||\nabla|^{s-\frac{1}{2}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-\frac{1}{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\\
&+\left(\|P\|_{L^{\infty}}+\||\nabla|^{s-\frac{1}{2}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-\frac{1}{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\mathscr{R}^{\perp}\Omega\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\\
\end{split}

We observe that in view of
Definition

2.4
,

\mathscr{R}^{\perp}\cdot(\Omega)=|\nabla|^{-1}{\rm curl\,}\Omega=|\nabla|^{-1}G({\bf u})

This and (
2.3
) implies

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s-1}\left(\nabla P^{T}\,P+P\Omega P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{s-\frac{1}{2}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-\frac{1}{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}\\
&+\||\nabla|^{s-\frac{1}{2}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-\frac{1}{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\left((1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\sigma})\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\,\left(1+\||\nabla|^{s-\frac{1}{2}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-\frac{1}{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\\
&+\left(1+\||\nabla|^{s-\frac{1}{2}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-\frac{1}{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}G({\bf u})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\\
\end{split}

With the help of (
2.14
) and (
3.3
) we arrive at

(3.6)

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s-1}\left(\Omega_{P}^{a}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\left(1+\||\nabla|^{s-\frac{1}{2}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-\frac{1}{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\left(\||\nabla|^{s-\frac{1}{2}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-\frac{1}{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}\right)\\
\end{split}

On the other hand, for any
s>1

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\approx&\||\nabla|^{s-1}\nabla P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}=\||\nabla|^{s-1}(\nabla P\,P^{T}P)\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\approx&\max_{\tilde{s}\in[0,s-1]}\||\nabla|^{s-1-\tilde{s}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-\tilde{s}-1}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{\tilde{s}}(\nabla P\,P^{T})\|_{L^{\frac{d}{\tilde{s}+1}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\left(\|P\|_{L^{\infty}}+\||\nabla|^{s-1}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-1}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\,\||\nabla|^{s-1}(\nabla P\,P^{T})\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\left(1+\|\||\nabla|^{s-1}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-1}}}\right)\,\left(\||\nabla|^{s-1}\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\left(\|P\|_{L^{\infty}}+\||\nabla|^{s-1}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-1}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)^{2}\,\||\nabla|^{s-1}\Omega\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\\
\lesssim&\left(1+\|\||\nabla|^{s-1}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-1}}}\right)\,\left(\||\nabla|^{s-1}\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\left(\|P\|_{L^{\infty}}+\||\nabla|^{s-1}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-1}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)^{2}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\Omega\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:Omegacond:growth},\eqref{eq:411Ssmall}}{\lesssim}&\left(1+\||\nabla|^{s-1}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-1}}}\right)^{3}\left(\||\nabla|^{s-1}\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\\
\end{split}

Applying (
3.6
) we then have

(3.7)

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\left(1+\||\nabla|^{s-\frac{1}{2}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-\frac{1}{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{4}\right)\,\left(\||\nabla|^{s-\frac{1}{2}}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-\frac{1}{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\\
\end{split}

This implies that for any
s\in[1,\frac{d-2}{2}]
,

(3.8)

\||\nabla|^{s}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

Indeed, for
s=\frac{3}{2}
this follows from (
3.2
) and (
2.3
) and (
3.7
), for
s>\frac{3}{2}
this follows by induction and (
3.7
).

This establishes (
3.5
). Plugging (
3.8
) into (
3.6
), we also have

\||\nabla|^{s-1}\left(\Omega_{P}^{a}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\underbrace{\left(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)}_{\overset{\eqref{eq:411Ssmall}}{\lesssim}2}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}\\

This implies (
3.4
).

∎

Similarly we also observe the following estimates

Lemma 3.3
.

Under the assumptions of
Lemma

3.1
, for
\varepsilon
possibly smaller, we also have

\|\nabla P\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|\Omega\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

and

\max_{a\in\{1,\ldots,d\}}\|\Omega_{P}^{a}\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|\Omega\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|\Omega\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|\mathscr{R}^{\perp}\Omega\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

Proof.

We have

\operatorname{div}(P\nabla P^{T}+P\Omega P^{T})=0\quad\text{in $\mathbb{R}^{d}$}

and thus

\mathscr{R}\cdot(P\nabla P^{T}+P\Omega P^{T})=0.

Then

\begin{split}\|\nabla P\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim&\|\mathscr{R}^{\perp}(P\nabla P^{T}+P\Omega P^{T})\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|\Omega\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&[P]_{BMO}\|\nabla P\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+(1+[P]_{BMO})\|\Omega\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\varepsilon\,\|\nabla P\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|\Omega\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\end{split}

Choosing
\varepsilon\ll 1
we obtain the

\|\nabla P\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|\Omega\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

Also,

\begin{split}\|\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim&\|\mathscr{R}^{\perp}(P\nabla P^{T})\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|\mathscr{R}^{\perp}(P\Omega P^{T})\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&[P]_{BMO}\|\nabla P\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+[P]_{BMO}\|\Omega\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|\mathscr{R}^{\perp}\Omega\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\|\nabla P\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}+\|\nabla P\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|\Omega\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|\mathscr{R}^{\perp}\Omega\|_{L^{(p,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.\end{split}

With the previous estimate for
\nabla P
we conclude.
∎

Next we show that (
3.4
) etc. holds also for
\Omega_{P}^{0}
.

Lemma 3.4
.

Under the assumptions of
Lemma

3.2
, we actually have

(3.9)

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}

and

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

as well as (for any
q\in[1,\infty])

\|dP\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

and

\|\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\ \|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

Proof of
Lemma

3.4
.

We first observe that

\begin{split}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\partial_{t}P\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\leq&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\left(\Omega^{0}_{P}P\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\left(\Omega^{0}P\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\left(\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\Omega^{0}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\,\left(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}P\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\end{split}

Thus, with
Lemma

3.2
, and (
2.3
)

(3.10)

\begin{split}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.\end{split}

And we recall that we already have from
Lemma

3.2

(3.11)

\begin{split}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\nabla P\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.\end{split}

Next we consider the
\Omega^{0}_{P}
-estimate. We have

\begin{split}\Delta\Omega_{P}^{0}=&\sum_{a=1}^{d}\partial_{aa}\Omega_{P}^{0}\\
=&-\sum_{a=1}^{d}\underbrace{\partial_{at}\Omega_{P}^{a}}_{=0}+\sum_{a=1}^{d}\partial_{a}\left(\partial_{t}\Omega_{P}^{a}+\partial_{a}\Omega_{P}^{0}\right)\\
\end{split}

From the definition of
\Omega_{P}
, (
3.1
),

(3.12)

\begin{split}&\partial_{a}\Omega_{P}^{0}+\partial_{t}(\Omega_{P}^{a})\\
=&\partial_{a}(\partial_{t}PP^{T}+P\Omega^{0}\,P^{T})+\partial_{t}(-\partial_{a}P\,P^{T}+P\Omega^{a}P^{T})\\
=&P\left(\partial_{a}\Omega^{0}+\partial_{t}\Omega^{a}\right)P^{T}\\
&+\partial_{t}P\partial_{a}P^{T}-\partial_{a}P\partial_{t}P^{T}\\
&+\partial_{a}P\,\Omega^{0}P^{T}\\
&-\partial_{t}P\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\\
&+P\Omega^{0}\,\partial_{a}P^{T}\\
&-\partial_{t}P\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\\
\end{split}

Thus, we may write

(3.13)

\begin{split}\Omega^{0}_{P}=&|\nabla|^{-1}\mathscr{R}_{a}(P\underbrace{\left(\partial_{a}\Omega^{0}+\partial_{t}\Omega^{a}\right)}_{=G_{1;a,0}({\bf u})}P^{T})\\
&+|\nabla|^{-1}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(\partial_{t}P\partial_{a}P^{T}-\partial_{a}P\partial_{t}P^{T}\right)\\
&+|\nabla|^{-1}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(\partial_{a}P\,\Omega^{0}P^{T}\right)\\
&-|\nabla|^{-1}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(\partial_{t}P\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\\
&+|\nabla|^{-1}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(P\Omega^{0}\,\partial_{a}P^{T}\right)\\
&-|\nabla|^{-1}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(\partial_{t}P\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\end{split}

Thus

(3.14)

\begin{split}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim&(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}P\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{d-4}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})})\left(\|G_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\|_{L^{\frac{d}{2}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}G_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\\
&+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\nabla P\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{d-2}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\partial_{t}P\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{d-2}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\Omega^{0}\|_{L^{2}}\left(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2]}}P\|_{L^{2}}\right)\\
\end{split}

So in particular we obtain with the help of (
3.11
), (
2.14
), (
3.3
),(
2.3
)

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}+\varepsilon\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}}\\

which together with (
3.10
), for
\varepsilon\ll 1
, implies

(3.15)

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

Plugging this again into (
3.14
), again with the help of (
3.11
), (
2.14
), (
3.3
),(
2.3
), we have

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}.

Similarly,

\begin{split}\|\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim&\|G_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{3},q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}P\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|\nabla P\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\Omega^{0}\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|\nabla P\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}P\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\ \|\Omega^{a}\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:G1cond:3}}{\lesssim}&\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}P\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\end{split}

On the other hand,

\|\partial_{t}P\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

we conclude that by the smallness assumption (
3.3
)

\|\partial_{t}P\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

and thus also

\|\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\ \|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,q)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

∎

Lemma 3.5
.

Under the assumptions of
Lemma

3.2
, we actually have

(3.16)

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}d\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}

and

(3.17)

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}ddP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

Proof.

We observe that

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}ddP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\partial_{tt}P\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

The first term is already estimated in
Lemma

3.4
. We then observe

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\partial_{tt}P\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
=&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\left(\partial_{tt}P\,P^{T}P\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
=&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\left(\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{t}P\,P^{T}\right)P\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\left(\partial_{t}P\,\partial_{t}P^{T}\,P\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
=&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\left(\partial_{t}\Omega_{P}^{0}\ P\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\left(\partial_{t}(P^{t}\Omega^{0}P)\ P\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\left(\partial_{t}P\,\partial_{t}P^{T}\,P\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}P\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})})\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\left(\partial_{t}\Omega_{P}^{0}\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\left(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{3}\right)\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}d\Omega^{0}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}P\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})})\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}\\
\end{split}

In view of
Lemma

3.4
, (
2.3
), and the smallness assumption (
3.3
), we obtain

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}ddP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}d\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

So it remains to estimate
d\Omega_{P}
. For
a\in\{1,\ldots,d\}
we use (
3.12
)

\begin{split}\partial_{t}\Omega^{a}_{P}=&\partial_{a}\Omega^{P}\\
&+P\left(G_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\right)P^{T}\\
&+\partial_{t}P\partial_{a}P^{T}-\partial_{a}P\partial_{t}P^{T}\\
&+\partial_{a}P\,\Omega^{0}P^{T}\\
&-\partial_{t}P\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\\
&+P\Omega^{0}\,\partial_{a}P^{T}\\
&-\partial_{t}P\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\\
\end{split}

and thus with
Lemma

3.4
, (
2.14
), (
2.3
), and the smallness assumption (
3.3
),

\max_{a\in\{1,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}d\Omega^{a}_{P}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}))}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}.

So what we actually only need to estimate is
\partial_{t}\Omega^{0}_{P}
, and we observe again from (
3.12
)

(3.18)

\begin{split}|\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\partial_{t}\Omega^{0}_{P}=&|\nabla|^{\frac{d-6}{2}}\mathscr{R}_{a}\partial_{t}(P\underbrace{\left(\partial_{a}\Omega^{0}+\partial_{t}\Omega^{a}\right)}_{=G_{1;a,0}({\bf u})}P^{T})\\
&+|\nabla|^{\frac{d-6}{2}}\mathscr{R}_{a}\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{t}P\partial_{a}P^{T}-\partial_{a}P\partial_{t}P^{T}\right)\\
&+|\nabla|^{\frac{d-6}{2}}\mathscr{R}_{a}\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{a}P\,\Omega^{0}P^{T}\right)\\
&-|\nabla|^{\frac{d-6}{2}}\mathscr{R}_{a}\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{t}P\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\\
&+|\nabla|^{\frac{d-6}{2}}\mathscr{R}_{a}\partial_{t}\left(P\Omega^{0}\,\partial_{a}P^{T}\right)\\
&-|\nabla|^{\frac{d-6}{2}}\mathscr{R}_{a}\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{t}P\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:OmegaPalpha}}{=}&|\nabla|^{\frac{d-6}{2}}\mathscr{R}_{a}\partial_{t}(P\,G_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\,P^{T})\\
&+|\nabla|^{\frac{d-6}{2}}\mathscr{R}_{a}\partial_{t}\left(\Omega_{P}^{0}\partial_{a}P^{T}-\partial_{a}P\,P^{T}\Omega_{P}^{0}\right)\\
&+|\nabla|^{\frac{d-6}{2}}\mathscr{R}_{a}\partial_{t}\left(-P\Omega^{0}\,\partial_{a}P^{T}+\partial_{a}P\,\Omega^{0}P^{T}\right)\\
&+|\nabla|^{\frac{d-6}{2}}\mathscr{R}_{a}\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{a}P\,\Omega^{0}P^{T}\right)\\
&+|\nabla|^{\frac{d-6}{2}}\mathscr{R}_{a}\partial_{t}\left(P\Omega^{0}\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\\
&-|\nabla|^{\frac{d-6}{2}}\mathscr{R}_{a}\partial_{t}\left(\Omega^{0}_{P}P\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\\
&+|\nabla|^{\frac{d-6}{2}}\mathscr{R}_{a}\partial_{t}\left(P\Omega^{0}\,\partial_{a}P^{T}\right)\\
&+|\nabla|^{\frac{d-6}{2}}\mathscr{R}_{a}\partial_{t}\left(\Omega^{0}\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\\
&-|\nabla|^{\frac{d-6}{2}}\mathscr{R}_{a}\partial_{t}\left(\Omega^{0}_{P}P\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\end{split}

Then we have
Lemma

3.4
, (
2.14
), (
2.3
), and the smallness assumption (
3.3
),

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\partial_{t}\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\left(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}\right)\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}\\
&+\left(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}\right)\,\left(\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\Omega\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\partial_{t}\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}\\
&+\varepsilon\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\partial_{t}\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\end{split}

For
\varepsilon
sufficiently small we can absorb the last term and obtain the desired result, (
3.16
) and (
3.17
).
∎

Lemma 3.6
.

Let
s\in[0,\frac{d-{2}}{2}{]}
,
p\in(\frac{d}{d+s-1},\frac{d}{s})
then, assuming (
3.3
) is satisfied,

(3.19)

\||\nabla|^{s}\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{s}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

and if
s\geq 1
and
p>\frac{d}{d+s-2}

(3.20)

\||\nabla|^{s-1}d\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{s}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

Also

(3.21)

\||\nabla|^{s}dP\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{s}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

and if
s\geq 1

(3.22)

\||\nabla|^{s-1}ddP\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{s}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

We also have as a variation of (
3.20
), cf. (
2.15
),

(3.23)

\|d\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{3},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

Proof.

We begin again with the
spatial part

\Omega_{P}^{\prime}=(\Omega_{P}^{a})_{a=1\ldots,d}
and then observe that since
\operatorname{div}(\Omega_{P}^{\prime})=0
,

\begin{split}\||\nabla|^{s}\Omega_{P}^{\prime}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{s}\mathscr{R}^{\perp}\left(\Omega_{P}^{\prime}\right)\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{s}[\mathscr{R}^{\perp},P](\nabla P^{T})\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s+1}\mathscr{R}^{\perp}\left(P\Omega^{\prime}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\left(\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}\right)\,\left(\||\nabla|^{s+1}P\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s}\Omega^{\prime}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\\
&+\||\nabla|^{s-1}{\rm curl\,}(\Omega^{\prime})\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\end{split}

With
Lemma

3.4
, (
2.1
), (
2.13
), and (
3.3
), we find for any
s\in(0,\frac{d}{2})
,

(3.24)

\begin{split}\||\nabla|^{s}\Omega_{P}^{\prime}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\left(\||\nabla|^{s+1}P\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\end{split}

Moreover,

\nabla P=\Omega_{P}^{\prime}\,P-P\Omega^{\prime}

so that again with the same estimates as above, whenever
p<\frac{d}{s}
,

(3.25)

\begin{split}\||\nabla|^{s+1}P\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim&\max_{t\in[0,s]}\||\nabla|^{t}\Omega_{P}^{\prime}\|_{L^{\frac{pd}{d-(s-t)p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{s-t}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\max_{t\in[0,s]}\in\||\nabla|^{t}\Omega^{\prime}\|_{L^{\frac{pd}{d-(s-t)p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{s-t}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s-t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})})\left(\||\nabla|^{s}\Omega_{P}^{\prime}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{p}{p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\end{split}

Combining (
3.24
) and (
3.25
), together with
Lemma

3.4
and (
3.3
) we readily obtain (
3.20
) and the spatial version of (
3.21
):

(3.26)

\begin{split}\||\nabla|^{s+1}P\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{s}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\max_{a\in\{1,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{s}\Omega_{P}^{a}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{s}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\end{split}

With the help of (
3.13
) we obtain under the assumption that
p>\frac{d}{d-(1-s)}
, for
\bar{s}:=\max\{s-1,0\}
and
\underline{s}:=-\min\{s-1,0\}
(observe that we can choose the spatial objects for the
L^{p}
-norm and can apply (
3.26
))

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s}\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{\bar{s}}\left(PG_{1;a,0}({\bf u})P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{dp}{d+\underline{s}p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\max_{a\in\{1,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{\bar{s}}\left(\partial_{t}P\partial_{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{dp}{d+\underline{s}p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\max_{a\in\{1,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{\bar{s}}\left(\partial_{t}P\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{dp}{d+\underline{s}p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\max_{a\in\{1,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{\bar{s}}\left(\partial_{a}P\,\Omega^{0}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{dp}{d+\underline{s}p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\max_{a\in\{1,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{\bar{s}}\left(P\Omega^{0}\,\partial_{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{dp}{d+\underline{s}p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\max_{a\in\{1,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{\bar{s}}\left(\partial_{t}P\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{dp}{d+\underline{s}p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\left(\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\Omega\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\left(\||\nabla|^{s}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s+1}P\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|\Omega^{\prime}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\||\nabla|^{s}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.\end{split}

Since

\partial_{t}P=\Omega_{P}^{0}\,P+P\Omega^{0},

we can argue as before, and we have established (
3.19
) and (
3.21
).

We continue by using (
3.18
). Let
\bar{s}:=\max\{s-2,0\}
and
\underline{s}:=-\min\{s-2,0\}
(observe that
\bar{s}\leq s-1
since
s\geq 1
by assumption), then we have for
p>\frac{d}{d+s-2}
, again by choosing the
L^{p}
-norms carefully,

(3.27)

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s-1}\partial_{t}\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{\bar{s}}\partial_{t}(P\,G_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\,P^{T})\|_{L^{\frac{dp}{d+\underline{s}p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\||\nabla|^{\bar{s}}\partial_{t}\left(\Omega_{P}^{0}\partial_{a}P^{T}-\partial_{a}P\,P^{T}\Omega_{P}^{0}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{dp}{d+\underline{s}p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\||\nabla|^{\bar{s}}\partial_{t}\left(-P\Omega^{0}\,\partial_{a}P^{T}+\partial_{a}P\,\Omega^{0}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{dp}{d+\underline{s}p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\||\nabla|^{\bar{s}}\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{a}P\,\Omega^{0}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{dp}{d+\underline{s}p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\||\nabla|^{\bar{s}}\partial_{t}\left(P\Omega^{0}\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{dp}{d+\underline{s}p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\||\nabla|^{\bar{s}}\partial_{t}\left(\Omega^{0}_{P}P\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{dp}{d+\underline{s}p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\||\nabla|^{\bar{s}}\partial_{t}\left(P\Omega^{0}\,\partial_{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{dp}{d+\underline{s}p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\||\nabla|^{\bar{s}}\partial_{t}\left(\Omega^{0}\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{dp}{d+\underline{s}p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\||\nabla|^{\bar{s}}\partial_{t}\left(\Omega^{0}_{P}P\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{dp}{d+\underline{s}p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|G_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|\partial_{t}G_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\|_{L^{\frac{dp}{d+\underline{s}p}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\left(\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}d\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}d\Omega\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\cdot\\
&\quad\,\cdot\left(\||\nabla|^{s}\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s+1}P\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:411SSfullLqdd1},\eqref{eq:411SSfullLqddP1}}{\lesssim}&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\||\nabla|^{s}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\end{split}

Observing (
3.13
) and choosing again wisely the
L^{p}
-norms and (
3.17
) we conclude

\||\nabla|^{s-1}\partial_{t}\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{s-1}\partial_{t}\Omega_{P}^{0}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\||\nabla|^{s}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

This establishes (
3.20
).

For (
3.22
) we observe again that

\begin{split}\partial_{tt}P=&\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}P\,P^{T})P-(\partial_{t}P\,\partial_{t}P^{T})P\\
=&\partial_{t}(\Omega^{0}_{P})P+\partial_{t}(P\Omega^{0}P^{T})\,P-(\partial_{t}P\,\partial_{t}P^{T})P\\
\end{split}

Then we have similar to the arguments above, making also full use of (
3.21
),

\||\nabla|^{s-1}ddP\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\overset{\eqref{eq:411SSfullLqddP1}}{\lesssim}\||\nabla|^{s}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

Lastly, for (
3.23
), we slightly adapt the arguments e.g. as in (
3.27
). By collecting only
dP
and
\Omega_{P}
-terms (without additional derivatives) in
L^{(2d,2)}
-norms, we find

\begin{split}&\|\partial_{t}\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{3},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}(P\,G_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\,P^{T})\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{5},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\left(\Omega_{P}^{0}\partial_{a}P^{T}-\partial_{a}P\,P^{T}\Omega_{P}^{0}\right)\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{5},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\left(-P\Omega^{0}\,\partial_{a}P^{T}+\partial_{a}P\,\Omega^{0}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{5},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{a}P\,\Omega^{0}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{5},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\left(P\Omega^{0}\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{5},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\left(\Omega^{0}_{P}P\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{5},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\left(P\Omega^{0}\,\partial_{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{5},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\left(\Omega^{0}\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{5},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\left(\Omega^{0}_{P}P\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{5},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&(1+\|\partial_{t}P\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})})\,\|dG_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{5},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\left(\|d\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\frac{d}{2}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|d\Omega\|_{L^{\frac{d}{2}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|d|\nabla|P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{2}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\left(\|dP\|_{L^{(2d,2)}}+\|\Omega\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\\
\lesssim&\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.\end{split}

In the second to last inequality we used (
3.3
) to avoid writing squares of
L^{d}
-norms.
In the last inequality we used (
3.21
),(
3.3
),(
2.15
),(
2.3
),(
2.4
).

The other terms of
d\Omega_{P}
are similar.

We can conclude.
∎

Lemma 3.7

(
L^{\infty}
-estimates)
.

Under the assumptions of
Lemma

3.2
we have

\|\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}.

Proof.

We have for
b\in\{1,\ldots,n\}

\begin{split}\Delta\Omega_{P}^{b}=&\sum_{a=1}^{d}\partial_{aa}\Omega_{P}^{b}\\
=&\sum_{a=1}^{d}\underbrace{\partial_{ab}\Omega_{P}^{a}}_{=0}-\sum_{a=1}^{d}\partial_{a}\left(\partial_{b}\Omega_{P}^{a}-\partial_{a}\Omega_{P}^{b}\right)\\
\end{split}

and thus

|\nabla|\Omega_{P}^{b}=c\sum_{a=1}^{d}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(\partial_{b}\Omega_{P}^{a}-\partial_{a}\Omega_{P}^{b}\right)

Similar to (
3.12
), using (
3.1
)

(3.28)

\begin{split}&\partial_{a}\Omega_{P}^{b}{-}\partial_{b}(\Omega_{P}^{b})\\
=&\partial_{a}(-\partial_{b}PP^{T}+P\Omega^{b}\,P^{T})-\partial_{b}(-\partial_{a}P\,P^{T}+P\Omega^{a}P^{T})\\
=&P\left(\partial_{a}\Omega^{b}{-}\partial_{b}\Omega^{a}\right)P^{T}\\
&-\partial_{b}P\partial_{a}P^{T}+\partial_{a}P\partial_{b}P^{T}\\
&+\partial_{a}P\,\Omega^{b}P^{T}-\partial_{b}P\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\\
&+P\Omega^{b}\,\partial_{a}P^{T}-\partial_{b}P\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\\
\end{split}

Consequently, using also (
2.4
),

\begin{split}\||\nabla|\Omega_{P}^{b}\|_{L^{(d,1)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim&\|\partial_{b}\Omega_{P}^{a}-\partial_{a}\Omega_{P}^{b}\|_{L^{(d,1)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\||DP|^{2}\|_{L^{(d,1)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||DP||\Omega|\|_{L^{(d,1)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:Omegacond:growth2d}}{\lesssim}&\||\nabla|P\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}+\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}\\
\overset{L.~\ref{la:L2destP},\eqref{eq:Omegacond:growth2d},\eqref{eq:411Ssmall}}{\lesssim}&\|\Omega\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}+\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}.\end{split}

By Sobolev embedding
W^{1,(d,1)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})\subset C^{0}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
,

\max_{b\in\{1,\ldots,d\}}\|\Omega_{P}^{b}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}.

We also have

\begin{split}\|\Omega_{P}^{a}\|_{L^{2d,2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim&\||\nabla|\Omega_{P}^{a}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{3},2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\end{split}

Time estimates
: Again we write

\begin{split}|\nabla|\Omega_{P}^{0}=&-c\sum_{a=1}^{d}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(\partial_{0}\Omega_{P}^{a}+\partial_{a}\Omega_{P}^{0}\right)\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:asdcioxckj1}}{=}&-c\sum_{a=1}^{d}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(P\left(G_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\right)P^{T}\right)\\
&-c\sum_{a=1}^{d}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(\partial_{t}P\partial_{a}P^{T}-\partial_{a}P\partial_{t}P^{T}\right)\\
&-c\sum_{a=1}^{d}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(\partial_{a}P\,\Omega^{0}P^{T}\right)\\
&-c\sum_{a=1}^{d}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(-\partial_{t}P\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\\
&-c\sum_{a=1}^{d}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(P\Omega^{0}\,\partial_{a}P^{T}\right)\\
&-c\sum_{a=1}^{d}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(-\partial_{t}P\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\\
\end{split}

and thus

\begin{split}\||\nabla|\Omega_{P}^{0}\|_{L^{(d,1)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim&\|G_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\|_{L^{d,1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|dP\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\|\nabla P\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|dP\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\|\Omega\|_{L^{2d,2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.\end{split}

With (
2.17
),
Lemma

3.4
, (
2.4
), (
3.3
),

\begin{split}\||\nabla|\Omega_{P}^{0}\|_{L^{(d,1)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}.\end{split}

We conclude

\|\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}.

∎

Lemma 3.8
.

Let
d\geq 4
. For
\sigma\in[0,1]
such that
(1-\sigma)\in{(}\frac{2(s_{1}-1)}{d-2},1]
and
s_{1}\in[0,\frac{d-2}{2}{]}
we have

(3.29)

\||\nabla|^{s_{1}}\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2-(1-\sigma)}\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}

If additionally
s_{1}\geq 1
and assuming additionally that
{1-\sigma>\frac{2}{d-2}}
(in particular: assume
d\geq 5
)

(3.30)

\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}d\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2-(1-\sigma)}\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}

Note that both estimates have a replacement that holds for
s_{1}=\frac{d-2}{2}
and
\sigma=0
in
Lemma

3.5
.

A special limit estimate: for
1-\sigma\in[\frac{2}{d-2},1]
then

(3.31)

\|d\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{3-\sigma}}}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\sigma}\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}

Proof.

Spatial part of (
3.29
)
: Denoting by
\Omega_{P}^{\prime}:=(\Omega_{P}^{a})_{a=1,\ldots,n}
we have by assumption
\mathscr{R}\cdot\Omega_{P}^{\prime}=0
and thus

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}}\Omega_{P}^{a}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}}\mathscr{R}^{\perp}(\Omega_{P}^{\prime})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}}\mathscr{R}^{\perp}(P\nabla P^{T})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}+\||\nabla|^{s_{1}}\mathscr{R}^{\perp}(P\Omega P^{T})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\end{split}

For the first term we have

|\nabla|^{s_{1}}\mathscr{R}^{\perp}(P\nabla P^{T})=|\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}{\rm curl\,}(P\nabla P^{T}),

so that with
Lemma

3.4
(where we use
1-\sigma
instead of
\sigma
for the application of (
A.5
))

\begin{split}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}}\mathscr{R}^{\perp}(P\nabla P^{T})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\lesssim&\max_{a,b\in\{1,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}\left(\partial_{a}P\partial_{b}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:GN:234}}{\lesssim}&\||\nabla|P\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2-(1-\sigma)}\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}P\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2-(1-\sigma)}\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}\\
\end{split}

For the second term we have

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}}\mathscr{R}^{\perp}(P\Omega P^{T})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\max_{i,\ldots,m}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}}\left([\mathscr{R}^{\perp},P_{ij}](P_{ml}\Omega_{kl})\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
&+\max_{i,\ldots,m}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}}\left(P_{ij}[\mathscr{R}^{\perp},P_{ml}](\Omega_{kl}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
&+\max_{i,\ldots,m}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}}\left(P_{ij}P_{ml}\mathscr{R}^{\perp}\Omega_{kl}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\max_{i,\ldots,m}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}}\left([\mathscr{R}^{\perp},P_{ij}](P_{ml}\Omega_{kl})\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
&+\max_{t\in[0,s_{1}]}\max_{i,\ldots,m}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-t}P_{ij}\|_{L^{\frac{d}{s_{1}-t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{t}[\mathscr{R}^{\perp},P_{ml}](\Omega_{kl})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2t-\sigma}}}\\
&+\max_{i,\ldots,m}\max_{t_{1}+t_{2}+t_{3}\in[0,s_{1}]}\||\nabla|^{t_{1}}P_{ij}\|_{L^{\frac{d}{t_{1}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{t_{2}}P_{ij}\|_{L^{\frac{d}{t_{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{t_{3}}\mathscr{R}^{\perp}\Omega_{kl}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2t_{3}-\sigma}}}\\
\end{split}

So estimating the extra terms by
\||\nabla|^{d}P_{ij}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{d}{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\leq 1
and
\|P\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq 1
we arrive at

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}}\mathscr{R}^{\perp}(P\Omega P^{T})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\max_{i,\ldots,m}\max_{t_{1}+t_{2}+t_{3}\in[0,s_{1}]}\||\nabla|^{t_{3}}\mathscr{R}^{\perp}\Omega_{kl}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2t_{3}-\sigma}}}\\
&+\max_{i,\ldots,m}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}}\left([\mathscr{R}^{\perp},P_{ij}](P_{ml}\Omega_{kl})\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
&+\max_{t\in[0,s_{1}]}\max_{i,\ldots,m}\,\||\nabla|^{t}[\mathscr{R}^{\perp},P_{ml}](\Omega_{kl})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2t-\sigma}}}\\
\end{split}

Observe that since
(1-\sigma)\in{(}\frac{2(s_{1}-1)}{d-2},1]
then
(1-\sigma)\in{(}\frac{2(t-1)}{d-2},1]
for any
t\leq s_{1}
. Thus, by the curl-condition for the admissibility of
\Omega
,

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{t_{3}}\mathscr{R}^{\perp}\Omega_{kl}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2t_{3}-\sigma}}}\\
\approx&\||\nabla|^{t_{3}-1}{\rm curl\,}\Omega_{kl}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2t_{3}-\sigma}}}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:G1cond:GN1spatial}}{\lesssim}&\||\nabla|{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2-(1-\sigma)}\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}.\end{split}

From (
A.7
) we have

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}}\left([\mathscr{R}^{\perp},P_{ij}](P_{ml}\Omega_{kl})\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\left(\||\nabla|P\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\sigma}+\|P_{ml}\Omega_{kl}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\sigma}\right)\left(\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}P\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\left(P_{ml}\Omega_{kl}\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}\right)\\
\lesssim&\left(\||\nabla|P\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\sigma}+\|\Omega\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\sigma}\right)\left(\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}P\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}+\max_{t\in[0,\frac{d-2}{2}]}\||\nabla|^{t}P\|_{L^{\frac{d}{t}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}-t}\Omega\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{d-t2}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}\right)\\
\lesssim&\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\sigma}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}\\
\end{split}

In the last step we used
Lemma

3.4
, (
2.4
), the smallness of
\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}du\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}
to estimate additional terms by
1
.
For exactly the same reasons we have

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}}\left([\mathscr{R}^{\perp},P_{ml}](\Omega_{kl})\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\sigma}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}\\
\end{split}

time part of (
3.29
)

Again we write

\begin{split}|\nabla|^{s_{1}}\Omega_{P}^{0}\overset{\eqref{eq:asdcioxckj1}}{=}&-c|\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}\sum_{a=1}^{d}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(P\left(G_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\right)P^{T}\right)\\
&-c\sum_{a=1}^{d}|\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(\partial_{t}P\partial_{a}P^{T}-\partial_{a}P\partial_{t}P^{T}\right)\\
&-c\sum_{a=1}^{d}|\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(\partial_{a}P\,\Omega^{0}P^{T}\right)\\
&-c\sum_{a=1}^{d}|\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(-\partial_{t}P\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\\
&-c\sum_{a=1}^{d}|\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(P\Omega^{0}\,\partial_{a}P^{T}\right)\\
&-c\sum_{a=1}^{d}|\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}\mathscr{R}_{a}\left(-\partial_{t}P\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\\
\end{split}

From (
A.5
) (again, for
1-\sigma
in place of
\sigma
) combined yet again with the observation that

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}(PA)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\left(\|P\|_{L^{\infty}}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}P\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}a\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}A\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

we readily find

\begin{split}\|\Omega_{P}^{0}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}\left(P\left(G_{1;}({\bf u})\right)P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
&+\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\sigma}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}.\end{split}

For the first term, if
s_{1}\leq 1
we use Sobolev embedding

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}\left(P\left(G_{1;}({\bf u})\right)P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\|P\left(G_{1;}({\bf u})\right)P^{T}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{3-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\|G_{1;}({\bf u})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{3-\sigma}}}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:G1cond:GN1spatial}}{\lesssim}&\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2-(1-\sigma)}\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}.\end{split}

If
s_{1}\geq 1
, using again
Lemma

3.4
and the smallness
\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}du\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}
to estimate unnecessary terms by one,

(3.32)

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}\left(P\left(G_{1;}({\bf u})\right)P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\left(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}P\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}P\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}\right)\,\max_{t\in[0,s_{1}]}\||\nabla|^{t-1}\left(G_{1;}({\bf u})\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2t-\sigma}}}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:G1cond:GN1spatial}}{\lesssim}&\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2-(1-\sigma)}\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}.\end{split}

This concludes the proof of (
3.29
).

As for (
3.30
) we first observe that with the arguments above, using (
3.12
), (
3.28
),
Lemma

3.4
, (
2.18
), (
3.3
)

(3.33)

\begin{split}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}d\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}\partial_{t}\Omega_{P}^{0}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
&+\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}PG_{1;}({\bf u})P^{T}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
&+\max_{\alpha,\beta\in\{0,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}(\partial_{\alpha}P\,\partial_{\beta}P^{T})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
&+\max_{\alpha,\beta\in\{0,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}(\partial_{\alpha}P\,\Omega^{\beta}P^{T})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
&+\max_{\alpha,\beta\in\{0,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}(P\,\Omega^{\beta}\partial_{\alpha}P^{T})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}\partial_{t}\Omega_{P}^{0}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}+\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2-(1-\sigma)}\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}.\end{split}

That is, it remains to estimate

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}\partial_{t}\Omega_{P}^{0}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:asdcioxckj12}}{\lesssim}&\max_{a\in\{1,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-2}\partial_{t}(PG_{1;a,0}({\bf u})P^{T})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
&+\max_{\alpha,\beta\in\{0,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-2}\left(\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{t}P\,\partial_{\beta}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
&+\max_{\alpha,\beta\in\{0,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-2}\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{\alpha}P\,\Omega^{\beta}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
&+\max_{\alpha,\beta\in\{0,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-2}\partial_{t}\left(P\,\Omega^{\beta}\,\partial_{\alpha}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\end{split}

Arguing similar to (
3.32
)

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-2}\partial_{t}(PG_{1;a,0}({\bf u})P^{T})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}\left(PG_{1;}({\bf u})P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{5-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2})\max_{t\in[2,s_{1}]}\||\nabla|^{t-2}dG_{1;}({\bf u})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2t-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:411Ssmall}}{\lesssim}&\max_{t\in[2,s_{1}]}\||\nabla|^{t-2}dG_{1;}({\bf u})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2t-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:G1cond:GN1}}{\lesssim}&\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}}^{1+\sigma}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}.\end{split}

The above makes sense if
s_{1}\geq 2
. If
s_{1}\leq 2
we obtain

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-2}\partial_{t}(PG_{1;a,0}({\bf u})P^{T})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\|dG_{1;}({\bf u})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:G1cond:GN1}}{\lesssim}&\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2-(1-\sigma)}\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}dd{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}\end{split}

Next, recall that
s_{1}\in[0,\frac{d-2}{2}]

If
s_{1}-2\leq 0
then for
\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}\in[0,1]
with
1-\sigma_{1}+1-\sigma_{2}=1-\sigma
,

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-2}\left(\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{t}P\,\partial_{\beta}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{2+2(s_{1}-1)+1-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{t}P\,\partial_{\beta}P^{T}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{4+1-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{t}P\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{4-\sigma_{1}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|\partial_{\beta}P^{T}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{2-\sigma_{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:411SSfullLqddP}}{\lesssim}&\|\partial_{\alpha}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{3+(1-\sigma_{1})}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|dP^{T}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+(1-\sigma_{2})}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:GN:1}}{\lesssim}&\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\sigma_{1}}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma_{1}}\,\|dP\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\sigma_{2}}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}dP\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma_{2}}\\
\overset{\lx@cref{creftypecap~refnum}{la:fullOmegaPLdest}}{\lesssim}&\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\sigma}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}.\end{split}

This works for
1-\sigma_{1}\in[\frac{2}{d-2},1]
, and by assumption we have
(1-\sigma)\in(\frac{2}{d-2},1]
, so we choose
1-\sigma_{1}
just a little bit smaller than
1-\sigma
(or equal, for that matter).

If
s_{1}-2\geq 0
, for
\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}\in[0,1]
such that
\sigma_{1}+\sigma_{2}=1-\sigma
,

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-2}\left(\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{t}P\,\partial_{\beta}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{2+2(s_{1}-1)+1-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\max_{t\in[0,s_{1}-2]}\||\nabla|^{t}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{t}P\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2(t+1)+\sigma_{1}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-2-t}\partial_{\beta}P^{T}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2(s_{1}-2-t)+\sigma_{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:411SSfullLqddP}}{\lesssim}&\max_{t\in[0,s_{1}-2]}\||\nabla|^{t+1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2(t+1)+\sigma_{1}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-2-t}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2(s_{1}-2-t)+\sigma_{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:GN:1}}{\lesssim}&\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma_{1}}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\sigma_{1}}\ \|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma_{2}}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\sigma_{2}}\\
=&\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\sigma}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}.\end{split}

For this to work we need that
\sigma_{1}\in[\frac{2(t+1)}{d-2},1]
and
\sigma_{2}\in[\frac{2(s_{1}-2-t)}{d-2},1]
. Since
\sigma_{1}+\sigma_{2}=(1-\sigma)\in{(}\frac{2(s_{1}-1)}{d-2},1]
this choice of
\sigma_{1}
,
\sigma_{2}
is possible.

The last term we consider for (
3.33
) is the following (the remaining term goes exactly the same) is

\begin{split}\max_{\alpha,\beta\in\{0,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-2}\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{\alpha}P\,\Omega^{\beta}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}}\\
\end{split}

Again, if
s_{1}-2\leq 0
then for
\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}\in[0,1]
with
1-\sigma_{1}+1-\sigma_{2}=1-\sigma
,

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-2}\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{\alpha}P\,\Omega^{\beta}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{2+2(s_{1}-1)+1-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{\alpha}P\,\Omega^{\beta}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{4+1-\sigma}}}\\
\lesssim&\|P^{T}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{t}P\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{4-\sigma_{1}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|\Omega^{\beta}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{2-\sigma_{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|P^{T}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{t}\Omega^{\beta}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{4-\sigma_{1}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|\partial_{\alpha}P\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{2-\sigma_{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}P^{T}\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|\Omega^{\beta}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{2-\sigma_{1}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|\partial_{\alpha}P\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{2-\sigma_{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:411SSfullLqddP},\eqref{eq:Omegacond:growthpq}}{\lesssim}&\|\partial_{\alpha}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{3+(1-\sigma_{1})}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+(1-\sigma_{2})}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{2-\sigma_{1}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{2-\sigma_{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:GN:1}}{\lesssim}&\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\sigma}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}\\
&+\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\sigma}\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}\\
\overset{\eqref{eq:411Ssmall}}{\lesssim}&\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\sigma}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}.\end{split}

Again by the choice of
\sigma
we can choose
\sigma_{1}
,
\sigma_{2}
accordingly to fit the assumptions of (
A.3
).

As for (
3.31
), we argue similar to (
3.23
).

\begin{split}&\|\partial_{t}\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{3-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}(P\,G_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\,P^{T})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{5-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\left(\Omega_{P}^{0}\partial_{a}P^{T}-\partial_{a}P\,P^{T}\Omega_{P}^{0}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{5-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\left(-P\Omega^{0}\,\partial_{a}P^{T}+\partial_{a}P\,\Omega^{0}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{5-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{a}P\,\Omega^{0}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{5-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\left(P\Omega^{0}\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{5-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\left(\Omega^{0}_{P}P\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{5-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\left(P\Omega^{0}\,\partial_{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{5-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\left(\Omega^{0}\,\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{5-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\left(\Omega^{0}_{P}P\Omega^{a}P^{T}\right)\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{5-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&(1+\|\partial_{t}P\|_{L^{d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})})\,\|dG_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{5-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\left(\|d\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{4-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|d\Omega\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{4-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|d|\nabla|P\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{4-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\left(\|dP\|_{L^{2d}}+\|\Omega\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\\
\lesssim&\|dG_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{5-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{4-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}},\end{split}

where in the last inequality we used (
3.3
),(
3.20
), (
2.1
), (
2.2
), (
3.21
)
If
d=3
we’d need
\sigma>0
to apply (
3.20
), but since we assume
d\geq 4
this is true for any
\sigma\in[0,1]
.

From (
2.16
), (
2.8
), (
2.9
), for
(1-\sigma)\in[\frac{2}{d-2},1]
,

\|dG_{1;a,0}({\bf u})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{5-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\sigma}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}

From (
A.3
), whenever
(1-\sigma)\in[\frac{2}{d-2},1]
,

\|d\nabla{\bf u}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{4-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\sigma}\ \||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}.

So we have shown that whenever
(1-\sigma)\in[\frac{2}{d-2},1]
,

\|\partial_{t}\Omega^{0}_{P}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{3-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1+\sigma}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{1-\sigma}

The other terms of
d\Omega_{P}
are similar, we can conclude.
∎

4.
A priori estimates: Proof of Theorem
1.1

Let
{\bf u}
be a solution to (
1.2
) as in the assumptions of
Theorem

1.1
.

In the following we take
P:\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(-T,T)\times\mathbb{R}\to SO(N)
from
Section

3
– which is possible for
\varepsilon
small enough, by the growth assumptions on
\Omega
, (
2.3
).

We compute

\begin{split}&\partial_{t}(P\partial_{t}{\bf u})-\operatorname{div}(P\nabla{\bf u})\\
=&\partial_{t}P\partial_{t}{\bf u}-\nabla P\,\nabla{\bf u}+P(\partial_{tt}{\bf u}-\Delta{\bf u})\\
=&\partial_{t}P\partial_{t}{\bf u}-\nabla P\,\nabla{\bf u}+P\Omega^{0}\partial_{t}{\bf u}+P\Omega\nabla{\bf u}\\
=&(\partial_{t}PP^{T}+P\Omega^{0}\,P^{T})\,P\partial_{t}{\bf u}+(-\nabla P\,P^{T}+P\Omega P^{T})\cdot P\nabla{\bf u}+PF({\bf u})\\
\end{split}

With the definition (
3.1
) this becomes

(4.1)

\partial_{t}(P\partial_{t}{\bf u})-\operatorname{div}(P\nabla{\bf u})=\Omega_{P}\cdot Pd{\bf u}+PF({\bf u}).

Now we perform essentially a space-time Hodge decomposition. Solve

(4.2)

\begin{cases}\left(\partial_{tt}-\Delta\right){\bf v}:=\Omega_{P}\cdot Pd{\bf u}+PF({\bf u})\\
{\bf v}(0)=0\\
\partial_{t}{\bf v}(0)=0\end{cases}

and set

(4.3)

W_{i}:=P_{ij}du^{j}-dv^{i}\quad\text{$i=1,\ldots N$}

We will compute the equation of
W_{i}
below, but first we observe the estimates for
v
we obtain from
Section

3
and the inversion of the wave operator in (
4.2
).

Lemma 4.1

(Estimate for
{\bf v}
)
.

Let
s\in(1,\frac{d^{2}-4d+1}{2(d-1)})
and
d\geq 4
. There exists
\varepsilon>0
such that if (
2.10
) holds
and
v
solves (
4.2
) then

(4.4)

\begin{split}&\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf v}\|_{C^{0}_{t}L^{2}_{x}((\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(-T,T))}+\||\nabla|^{s-1}{d}{\bf v}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(-T,T))}\\
\lesssim_{s}&\||\nabla|^{s-1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times(-T,T))}^{2}.\end{split}

Proof.

At a fixed point in time
t\in(-T,T)
we estimate

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}(\partial_{tt}-\Delta){\bf v}\|_{L^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\left(\Omega_{P}\cdot Pd{\bf u}\right)\|_{L^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\left(PF({\bf u})\right)\|_{L^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

We observe that

\begin{split}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\left(PF({\bf u})\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim&\left(\|P\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}P\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}F({\bf u})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.\end{split}

By admissibility of
F
,
Definition

2.2
(
2.7
), we have

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\left(PF({\bf u})\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\left(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d}{2}}P\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\right)\,\left(1+\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{\gamma}\right)\||\nabla|^{s-1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}

In view of
Lemma

3.4
, the smallness assumption (
2.10
) we have shown for suitably small
\varepsilon

(4.5)

\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\left(PF({\bf u})\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{s-1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}

On the other hand we compute, we will now show

(4.6)

\max_{\alpha,\beta\in\{0,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}d(\Omega_{P}^{\alpha}\,P^{T}\partial_{\beta}{\bf u})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\gamma\||\nabla|^{s-2}dd{\bf u}\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}.

and

(4.7)

\max_{\alpha,\beta\in\{0,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}(\Omega_{P}^{\alpha}\,P^{T}\partial_{\beta}{\bf u})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{s-1}d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(\frac{2d}{2s-1},2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2}.

Observe that this is slightly more general that what is needed here, but later for the equation in
Lemma

4.2
we also need the time-derivative.

In order to obtain (
4.6
), with the help of the Leibniz rule,

(4.8)

\begin{split}&\max_{\alpha,\beta\in\{0,\ldots,d\}}\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}d(\Omega_{P}^{\alpha}\,P\partial_{\beta}{\bf u})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
\lesssim&\|\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}d\left(Pd{\bf u}\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\|P\|_{L^{\infty}}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}d\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{d-1}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{2d}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\max_{s_{1}+s_{2}=\frac{d-2}{2},s_{1},s_{2}\geq 1}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}-1}d\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{s_{2}}(Pd{\bf u})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2s_{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
&+\max_{s_{1}+s_{2}=\frac{d-2}{2},s_{1},s_{2}\geq 1}\||\nabla|^{s_{1}}\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+2s_{1}-\sigma}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{s_{2}-1}d(Pd{\bf u})\|_{L^{\frac{2d}{1+\sigma+2s_{2}}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}.\end{split}

For the first line of (
4.8
) by
Lemma

3.7

\|\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|d{\bf u}\|_{L^{(2d,2)}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{2},

so that

\begin{split}&\|\Omega_{P}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\,\||\nabla|^{\frac{d-4}{2}}d\left(Pd{\bf u}\right)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\\
